2026-10-22T10:41:26.8683306Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Serve the Decode B1/B4/B6 bundle with the small-M dense GEMM geometry (B5) switched off

Run 39759800897 (9f93944, the full B1/B4/B5/B6 bundle) passed the gates but scored 7582.76 against main's 8356.18: ITL mean rose from 14.54 to 16.06 ms. The decode-0 profile attributes the whole regression to B5. With DG_SM90_1D2D_SMALL_M=1 the rebuilt host picks BLOCK_M 256 with BLOCK_N 32 (QKV) and 80 (O) for the 128-row Decode projections. That configuration takes 44.3 and 30.5 us per call, compared with 17.3 and 14.3 us for the stock BLOCK_M 64 configuration in run 39550627795: +43 us per layer, about +4.1 ms per Decode step.

Per MegaMoE call (one per layer), the other kernels improved against 39550627795, about -8.5 us per layer or about -0.8 ms per step in total:
- B4 metadata sharing: flash::prepare_varlen_num_blocks_kernel dropped from 1.03 to 0.09 calls per layer (3.65 -> 0.31 us). The FA3 verify kernel itself went from 50.8 to 49.0 us.
- B6 fusions: the unnamed RoPE/QK-norm kernels went from 14.4 to 12.8 us, and a separate 1.28 us unnamed kernel disappeared. The Triton router grew from 3.04 to 3.46 us.
- B1 MegaMoE prefetch: 199.2 -> 198.4 us.

Remove only DG_SM90_1D2D_SMALL_M=1 from the Decode exports. The host patch stays in image 39711569005, but without the variable it keeps the stock heuristic, so Decode returns to the 17.3/14.3 us configuration. Every other setting matches 9f93944:
- image 39711569005;
- the B1/B4/B6 overlays;
- the gate contents;
- hot-4 replication, cap 128, concurrency 192 and EAGLE3 steps 3 / draft 4;
- the job-local Triton cache.

If the per-layer savings hold, the Decode step should come out about 0.8 ms shorter than in 39550627795 (about +2.5%). One serving run with the usual normal benchmark and profile; no rebuild and no kernel bench.
